  <div  class="popup-content">
    
<form class="search-form" role="search" method="get" id="" action="https://www.group-ib.com/">
  <input type="search" placeholder="Search" name="s" id="search-input" value=""/>
  <button type="submit" class="search-button"><img src="/wp-content/themes/gib-theme/assets/images/search-icon.svg" alt="Search Icon"></button>
</form>   </div>

<style>
  .hidden {
    display: none;
  }
</style>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
  const openSearchPopupBtns = [...document.querySelectorAll('.open-search-popup')];
  const closeSearchPopupBtns = [...document.querySelectorAll('.close-search-popup')];
  const searchPopup = document.querySelector('.search-popup');

  const closePopup = () => {
    searchPopup.style.display = 'none';
    openSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
    closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.add('hidden'));
  };

  openSearchPopupBtns.forEach(openSearchPopupBtn => {
    openSearchPopupBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();

      searchPopup.style.display = 'block';
      openSearchPopupBtn.classList.add('hidden');
      closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('hidden'));
    });
  });

  closeSearchPopupBtns.forEach(closeSearchPopupBtn => {
    closeSearchPopupBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      closePopup();
    });
  });

  document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
    if (!searchPopup.contains(event.target) && !openSearchPopupBtns.some(btn => btn.contains(event.target)) && !closeSearchPopupBtns.some(btn => btn.contains(event.target))) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!searchPopup.contains(event.target)) {
          closePopup();
        }
      }, 100);
    }
  });
</script>    </div>

                            <h1 class="header__title title-h2 mb-24 mt-md-48 mt-lg-64 mt-xl-64">Challenge accepted</h1><p class="header__text mb-xs-48 mb-64 mb-lg-96 mb-xl-96">Detecting MaliBot, a fresh Android banking trojan, with a Fraud Protection solution</p><div class="post-info__date mb-24 mb-lg-32 mb-xl-48"><div class="body2">August 11, 2022 · <span id="reading-time"></span> min to read · Fraud Protection</div></div><div class="post-info__image mb-24 mb-lg-32 mb-xl-48"><picture><source srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/img-1440-3-min.jpg.webp" type="image/webp"><img data-no-lazy="" fetchpriority="high" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/img-1440-3-min.jpg" alt="" class="webpexpress-processed"></picture></div><div class="post-info__tags"><div class="post-info__tag body2">Android</div><div class="post-info__tag body2">MaliBot</div><div class="post-info__tag body2">Trojan</div></div>                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="blog-post-content" id="blog-post-content">
                    <div class="blog-post-content__section--text"><p>Flubot is dead, and the new evil is detected and crowned, the first of his name – <strong>MaliBot</strong>. Well, in fact this new <strong>MaliBot is a revised follower of another Android banking trojan – S.O.V.A. MaliBot malware</strong>, usually disguised as a cryptocurrency mining application, targets Android devices and uses overlay attacks to outfox MFA/2FA, capture messages and SMS, and steal banking and crypto credentials.</p>
<p>By this moment, <strong>it has mainly threatened financial sector companies in Spain and Italy</strong>. Obviously, it has an ominous ambition and surely still untapped potential to infuse into other industries and conquer new territories.</p>
<p>In this article we will tell in more detail what techniques exist against such attacks, and give you a live demonstration of <strong>how <a href="/products/fraud-protection/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Group-IB Fraud Protection</a> stops MaliBot or other similar threats</strong>.</p>
<div id="attachment_23427" style="width: 1153px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/1-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-23427" decoding="async" class="wp-image-23427 size-full webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201143%20450'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="MaliBot fraud dossier" width="1143" height="450" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/1-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-23427" decoding="async" class="wp-image-23427 size-full webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/1-min-1.png" alt="MaliBot fraud dossier" width="1143" height="450"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-23427" class="wp-caption-text">MaliBot fraud dossier</p></div>
<h2>Challenging anti-fraud professionals</h2>
<p>As you can clearly see, this malware is powerful and tricky, which makes it a real challenge to detect this attack and other similar to MaliBot malwares with an average anti-fraud tool. That’s actually why <strong>the financial sector is such fertile soil for threat actors successfully stealing money from banking and cryptocurrency accounts</strong>.</p>
<p><strong>MaliBot operators harness a variety of distribution campaigns</strong>: they promote cryptocurrency applications in the form of APKs that victims are supposed to download and install manually; they clone real projects like TheCryptoApp (1M+ downloads on Google Play Store); operators also use <a href="/resources/knowledge-hub/smishing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">smishing</a> (SMS phishing) and other methods to multiply their chances to succeed.</p>
<p>Even though the overall context looks scary, fear not because advanced solutions and tactics, developed to respond to similar web injection threats and this particular one, do exist, and one of them is almost at your doorstep.</p>
<h2>Proven tactics to respond</h2>
<p>There are at least<strong> two major countermeasures to leverage against such fraud attacks</strong>. According to your case, you have a choice between fraud intelligence and behavioral analysis. Let us explain to you what’s the difference and how it works.</p>
<p>The signature-based analysis utilizes the abilities of <strong>fraud intelligence</strong> to collect information about the application and compare its certification deviation to other application certificates. Once information about installed applications is gathered, it is essential to match them with already known trojan signatures.</p>
<p><strong>Here are some examples of suspicious Android permissions:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>PROCESS_OUTGOING_CALLS</li>
<li>SEND_SMS</li>
<li>WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE</li>
<li>READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE</li>
<li>RECEIVE_SMS</li>
</ul>
<p>Another way to thwart such attacks is a so-called <strong>behavioral analysis</strong> that</p>
<ul>
<li>comprises overlay or accessibility service detections;</li>
<li>realizes default SMS application monitoring;</li>
<li>captures the slightest deviations in the user&#8217;s behavior routine.</li>
</ul>
<p>Every tiny derogation matters here: the speed of movement, pressure on the screen while using the app, and other factors that could drop an alert about anomalies.</p>
<h2>Where intelligence meets analysis</h2>
<p><strong>Group-IB <a href="/products/fraud-protection/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Fraud Protection</a> is a solution that combines device fingerprinting, fraud intelligence, and behavioral analysis</strong> and already protects more than 300M+ web and mobile apps users worldwide against advanced digital threats, malware, payment fraud, <a href="/resources/knowledge-hub/social-engineering/">social engineering</a> attacks, and bad bots. The solution acts in real-time and across all digital channels.</p>
<p>Group-IB Fraud Protection utilizes the <a href="/products/threat-intelligence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">threat intelligence</a> insights collected by the <a href="/products/unified-risk-platform/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Unified Risk Platform</a> by Group-IB giving us the full fraud landscape visibility, and our professional expertise turns these insights into actionable anti-fraud strategies.</p>
<p>Better to see something once, so please <strong>check out this video demonstration of how MaliBot typically acts and what signals Group-IB Fraud Protection solution collects to detect the threat</strong>:</p>
</div>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    const postcontent = document.getElementById('blog-post-content');
    const h2counter = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2');

    for (i = 0; i < h2counter.length; i++) {
        const h2elementtag = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2')[i];
        const h2element = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2')[i].innerHTML;
        let postnavitem = document.createElement('a');
        postnavitem.classList.add('post-navigation__item', 'subtitle2', 'scrollto');
        postnavitem.innerHTML = h2element;
        const postnavlink = postnavitem.innerHTML;
        let str = postnavlink;
        str = str.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '');
        str = str.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s\u0400-\u04FF]/g, '');
        str = str.replace(/\s+/g, '-').toLowerCase();
        str = str.replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
        postnavitem.classList += ' ' + str;
        postnavitem.href = '#' + str;
        h2elementtag.id = str;
        document.getElementById('post-navigation__wrapper').appendChild(postnavitem);
    }

    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('h2');
    const navItems = document.querySelectorAll('.post-navigation__item');

    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        let current = '';
        sections.forEach((section) => {
            const sectionTop = section.offsetTop;
            const sectionHeight = section.clientHeight;
            if (pageYOffset >= sectionTop - sectionHeight / 10) {
                current = section.getAttribute('id');
            }
        });

        navItems.forEach((a) => {
            a.classList.remove('active');
            if (a.classList.contains(current)) {
                a.classList.add('active');
            }
        });
    });

    // SMOOTH SCROLL
    var $root = jQuery('html, body');


    jQuery('a[href^="#"].scrollto').click(function() {
        var href = jQuery.attr(this, 'href');
        var headerHeight = 100;

        $root.animate({
                scrollTop: jQuery(href).offset().top - headerHeight
            },
            300,
        );

        return false;
    });

    // SMOOTH SCROLL WITH NO HASH
    var $root = jQuery('html, body');


    jQuery('a[href^="#"].scrolltonohash').click(function() {
        var href = jQuery.attr(this, 'href');
        var headerHeight = 100;

        $root.animate({
                scrollTop: jQuery(href).offset().top - headerHeight
            },
            300
        );

        return false;
    });
</script>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    function readingTime() {
        const text = document.getElementById("blog-post-content").innerText;
        const wpm = 225;
        const words = text.trim().split(/\s+/).length;
        const time = Math.ceil(words / wpm);
        document.getElementById("reading-time").innerText = time;
    }
    readingTime();
</script>

    jQuery('.share-url__copy').click(function() {
        jQuery('.share-url__copy').addClass('success');

    function positioningSidebar() {
        let sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
        let sidebarHeight = sidebar.offsetHeight;
        let windowHeight = document.documentElement.clientHeight;
        if (windowHeight < (sidebarHeight + 48)) {
            let sidebarAuthors = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar__authors');
            let sidebarMorePosts = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar__more-posts');
            let authorsHeight = sidebarAuthors ? sidebarAuthors.offsetHeight : 0;
            let morePostsHeight = sidebarMorePosts ? sidebarMorePosts.offsetHeight : 0;
            let sidebarTop = authorsHeight + morePostsHeight + 140;
            jQuery(sidebar).css('top', -sidebarTop + 'px');
        } else {
            jQuery(sidebar).css('top', '72px');
        }
    }
    window.addEventListener('resize', positioningSidebar);
    positioningSidebar();

    if (localStorage.getItem('theme')) {
        if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'dark') {
            jQuery('body').addClass('theme--dark');
        } else if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'light') {
            jQuery('body').addClass('theme--white');
        }
    } else {
        localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        jQuery('body').addClass('theme--dark');
    }

    jQuery('.theme-toggle').click(function() {
        if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'dark') {
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
        } else {
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        }
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('no-transition');
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('theme--dark');
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('theme--white');
        setTimeout(() => {
            jQuery('body').toggleClass('no-transition');
        }, 250);
    });
    jQuery('.share-toggle').click(function() {
        jQuery('.share-toggle').toggleClass('active');
        jQuery('.share-widget').toggleClass('show');
    });
</script>
